# Project State

## Project Reference

See: .planning/PROJECT.md (updated 2026-08-04)

**Core value:** Glancing at the frame tells you, in real time, whether you'll make the next RER — while also being a satisfying ambient piece on the wall.
**Current focus:** Phase 06.6.4.1 — companion-page-by-page-ia-consolidation-full-page-by-page-vi (companion/UI track). Phase 9 — Diagonal band theme — is separately COMPLETE on the parallel firmware/panel track, merged from `origin/main` 2026-09-02 (see the Phase 7/8/9 entries below for that track's own history).

**06.6.4.1-01 executed (2026-09-01), wave 1's sole plan (no dependencies), closing D-01/D-04/D-07/D-09/D-14/D-20 — the phase's single write site for `companion/static/style.css`, landed first so waves 2-3's four page-module plans can run with zero file contention.** Task 1 deleted the `>=960px .config-form` two-column grid rule group outright (D-01's root-cause fix for Settings' broken Runway section — the grid was never designed to hold the fieldset's unwrapped elements; normal block flow now produces the single-column stack at every width) and added `.js [data-static-save-fallback] { display: none }` next to the existing `.js`-prefixed mobile-nav rules (D-04) — gated on the real `.js` class `nav-dropdown.js` sets unconditionally, so the unified dirty-bar becomes the sole visible save affordance only once client-side script is confirmed running, closing the bug where `dirty-state.js`'s `hidden`-toggle-only mechanism never touched the fallback button and both rendered at once. Task 2 added Airlines' illustration-gallery card system (D-14, UI-SPEC §7.1): `.illustration-grid` (200px auto-fill columns, wider than `.gallery-grid`'s 160px since these tiles carry a name and chip row too) plus `.airline-card` and its four BEM parts, carrying the 06.6.4 hairline-at-rest/shadow-on-hover contract verbatim and a neutral 11px/999px chip tier (`.airline-card__chip`) that adds zero new accent consumers. Task 3 added History's `.lightbox` system (D-20, §8.3) — a styled native `<dialog>`, deliberately elevated at rest (`box-shadow: var(--shadow-card-hover)`) as the phase's one documented exception to the hairline-at-rest contract, since it's a floating modal overlay rather than a page-flow card — plus `.lightbox__image`/`__caption`/`__note`/`::backdrop`; Health's `.sparkline-axis-label` (D-09, §5.3, `fill` not `color` since these are SVG text nodes, one sub-scale step below `.data-table th`'s 11px tier); and `.banner__pill` (D-07), placed directly after the `.banner` severity modifiers, declared byte-identical to `.airline-card__chip` on purpose (same primitive, two call sites, kept identical so a future consolidation is mechanical) and deliberately neutral- rather than severity-tinted, since the banner's own edge/dot already carries that signal. **06.6.4-01 executed (2026-09-01), the first of the phase's 6 plans (wave 1, no dependencies), closing D-03/D-03a/D-09.** `companion/static/style.css`'s `:root` block lost `--font-display-size` (24px) entirely — its two consumers, `.runway-card__number` and `.stat-tile__value`, moved onto the Emphasis role (`var(--font-body-size)` + `var(--weight-semibold)`), restoring the scale block's own claimed four sizes. All six card components (`.runway-card`, `.history-card`, `.stat-tile`, `.battery-trend-section`, `.page-section`, `.login-card`) were flipped from 06.6.1's shadow-at-rest treatment to a hairline-at-rest/shadow-on-hover contract: `border: 1px solid var(--color-border)`, `box-shadow: none`, `border-radius: var(--radius-control)` at rest, with a new (or, for `.stat-tile`, extended) `:hover, :focus-within` rule clearing the border and revealing `var(--shadow-card-hover)`. `.runway-card`'s hover rule is `:not(.runway-card--selected)`-scoped so a selected card's 2px accent border structurally cannot be cleared by hover (D-03a). Both obsolete dark-mode card-edge override blocks (`@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark)` and `html[data-ui-theme="dark"]`) were removed outright — redundant now that every card carries a real hairline in all themes, and load-bearing to remove rather than merely leave alone, since the attribute-selector variant outranked a bare `:hover` rule and would have silently pinned the border back on during hover for explicit-dark-mode users only. Every comment the change falsified was rewritten (file header, `.stat-tile`, `.page-section`, `.runway-card`, `.history-card`, `.login-card`, `.battery-trend-section`, `.site-title`). `.login-card` was deliberately included as a full sixth card, not an afterthought — excluding it would have left the pre-auth login page as the one surface still carrying resting relief. `--radius-card` itself was left untouched (still consumed by `.mobile-nav`, `.dirty-bar`, `.preview-frame`, none of this plan's scope, per RESEARCH.md's own Pattern 3). **06.6.4-02 executed (2026-08-31), the second of the phase's 6 plans (wave 2, depends on 01), closing D-01/D-01a/D-01b/D-01c/D-02.** `companion/static/style.css`'s base `button` rule lost both `min-height`/`min-width: 44px` (D-01's deliberate WCAG 2.5.5 AAA trade-off) and became a 30px-tall, 13px-semibold, 6px-cornered control with a near-invisible `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-text) 4.5%, transparent)` wash and a matching-strength hairline border (D-01b resting half) — the RESEARCH.md Pattern 1 idiom resolving D-01b/D-02's open "dark-mode analogue needed" question with one declaration instead of a per-theme override block. `button[type="submit"]` gained D-01a's inset-highlight-plus-shadow treatment at rest and a deeper pair on hover, both explicitly neutralizing `border-color` against the new base hairline. A new `button:active { transform: translateY(1px); }` rule (placed before `button:disabled`) gives every button a pressed state. `.logout-form button` and `.dirty-bar__cancel` moved onto the same quiet wash plus a new `box-shadow: none` (both are submit buttons that would otherwise inherit the primary inset highlight), and a new `button:hover` rule reveals a stronger wash/border for every quiet control, source-ordered after both overrides per the plan's own load-bearing placement requirement. A 44px floor register comment above the base `button` rule now exhaustively lists which selectors gave up the floor this phase (text buttons, nav links, theme-picker segments, filter input — the latter three not yet implemented, forward-referenced for plans 04/05) versus which keep it (hamburger toggle, native input/select, disclosure summary), stating the WCAG 2.5.8/2.5.5 criterion the trade-off rests on. `.copy-btn` became a 28x28 transparent glyph box (`border: none`, explicit `inline-flex` centring) with a new `::before { inset: -8px }` pseudo-element synthesizing a genuine 44x44 pointer/touch target — no accessibility trade-off — plus its own 7% hover wash; the stale "inherits the 44x44 floor from the base button rule" comment (a mechanism this plan's Task 1 removed) was rewritten to describe the actual pseudo-element mechanism, per RESEARCH.md's own flagged Pitfall 3. Kept the `.copy-btn` class name unchanged (no rename) — `history_page.py`'s `_copy_button_html()` builder and the DOM-contract test guards already reference it, the lower-blast-radius option per D-02's own "planner's call". **06.6.4-03 executed (2026-08-31), the third of the phase's 6 plans (wave 3, depends on 01/02), closing D-04/D-04a.** `companion/static/style.css`'s base `.banner` rule rebuilt per D-04: `display: flex; align-items: center; gap: 8px;` on `background: var(--color-dominant)` (the same card surface `06.6.4-01` gave every card), a 1px `var(--color-border)` shorthand border followed by a `border-left: 3px solid var(--color-accent)` restatement (order load-bearing — the shorthand resets all four sides, so the edge declaration must come after it or the alert edge silently collapses to 1px), and the 10%-wash `color-mix(...)` background removed outright rather than re-tinted. A new `.banner::before` pseudo-element (`content: ""; flex: none; width: 6px; height: 6px; border-radius: 50%`) supplies the status dot — deliberately not a reuse of the existing `.dot` class, since `companion/layout.py`'s `flash_banner()`/`anomaly_banner()` emit a single `<div>` with only escaped text as a child, so the dot has to be presentational. All three severity modifiers (`.banner--anomaly`/`.banner--warn`/`.banner--flash`) were reduced to `border-left-color` alone, each gaining a sibling `::before` rule for the matching dot colour — no severity's edge colour changed, only the wash-vs-dot mechanism. `.page-section.banner--anomaly` (Health's source-fault block, the combined card-and-banner selector's sole consumer) was reconciled for D-04a: edge narrowed 4px to 3px to match the new width, background moved from the retired wash to `--color-dominant`, and two new opt-outs — `display: block` plus `.page-section.banner--anomaly::before { display: none }` — stop the block inheriting Task 1's flex row and dot, since the source-fault block holds a heading plus several stacked paragraphs and would otherwise reflow into one horizontal row with the dot as its first flex item. **06.6.4-05 executed (2026-09-01), the fifth of the phase's 6 plans (wave 5, depends on 01/02/03/04), closing D-07/D-07a/D-08.** `companion/static/style.css`'s `.data-table th` lost its filled `background: var(--color-secondary)` block, becoming an 11px, letter-spaced (0.06em), uppercase, 70%-muted-text label row with a `var(--color-border)` bottom hairline — the same 70% muted strength the theme segments and merged-cell parts already use. `.data-table-wrap th` (the sticky rule) was edited in the same task per the plan's own load-bearing instruction (equal specificity, later source order — editing only the base rule would leave the header repainting solid the moment a long table scrolls): position/top/z-index unchanged, `background` moved from the elevated secondary surface to `var(--color-canvas)`, flagged in-file and in this SUMMARY as the one token in the phase RESEARCH.md never live-validated (a real-browser check against Airlines'/Health's in-card tables is an explicit item for the phase's closing checklist). `.data-table td` padding grew to `13px var(--space-md)`; new `tbody tr:last-child td { border-bottom: none }` and `tbody tr:hover` (4% text-tint) rules were added; the `.data-table tr.row-alt` zebra-stripe rule was deleted outright, with the three server-side sites still computing that class name (`companion/layout.py`'s `data_table()`, `history_page.py`, `airlines_page.py`) deliberately left untouched as documented inert markup. D-08's Clear-control fork (History's `<button type="button" data-filter-clear>` vs. Airlines' `<a href="#..." data-filter-clear>`) was resolved via a single `.filter-bar [data-filter-clear]` rule (plus `:hover`) keyed on the attribute both pages already emit — not a new shared class, as CONTEXT.md/RESEARCH.md both assumed would be needed — reaching pixel-identical output with zero edits to either page module and keeping Airlines' D-16 no-button-no-form constraint provably intact via an empty-diff gate on `companion/pages/`. `companion/test_view_pages.py` gained `_clear_control_shared_attribute_contract()`, a drift guard pinning that History's rendered output still carries `data-filter-clear`, `style.css` still styles it by attribute, and no competing class-keyed Clear-control rule exists; `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` bumped from its real on-disk baseline of 41 to 42. **Phase 06.6.2 (Companion UX, accessibility, and responsive hardening) — COMPLETE, 2026-08-31.** Inserted 2026-08-30 after Phase 06.6, seeded from `06.6.1-UX-AUDIT.md`'s follow-up recommendation. Originally scoped all 23 CONTEXT.md decisions (the full hardening + visual-redesign workstream); the planner determined that combined scope exceeded this project's own largest prior single-phase precedent and returned a phase-split recommendation, which the developer approved — the 9 per-page decisions (D-02–D-05, D-06's heading-dedup half, D-07–D-10, D-12, D-13, D-18, D-20, D-22, D-23) moved to the new Phase 06.6.3, and this phase kept the 8 shared-foundation decisions (D-01, D-06's accent-color half, D-11, D-14–D-17, D-19, D-21). Planned 2026-08-31: 8 plans across 7 waves (`06.6.2-01` through `06.6.2-08`), all executed the same day. Plan 08's closing checkpoint — the developer's own real local-browser pass across all 9 checklist groups (viewport/overflow, mobile-nav accessibility, contrast, sidebar, logout, login deep-link, severity, poll lock, keyboard-only) — approved with all 9 passing; two live-investigated observations during the checkpoint resolved as non-blocking (a History mobile-scroll preference already covered by 06.6.3's D-07, and a sidebar-sticky false alarm traced to testing the wrong URL). A post-checkpoint code review (`06.6.2-REVIEW.md`) found 1 Critical (skip-link target not focusable — CR-01) and 4 Warnings (nav-dropdown re-open race, missing hardening response headers, unauthenticated slow-body DoS, duplicate Health-severity DB reads); all 5 fixed via `/gsd-code-review --fix` (`06.6.2-REVIEW-FIX.md`), plus one mechanical `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` correction found while re-verifying. `gsd-verifier` confirmed 17/17 must-haves (`06.6.2-VERIFICATION.md`). One process note: the code-fixer agent's worktree-cleanup step mistakenly fast-forwarded the top-level `main` checkout (not this worktree) by 48 commits during its final merge step — caught immediately, fully recovered with the developer's explicit approval (`main` reset back to `188b00e`/`origin/main`, this worktree's branch fast-forwarded with the 5 fix commits, temp worktree/branch cleaned up), nothing was lost or pushed. Ready for Phase 06.6.3, which depends on this phase's tokens, `page_header()`, sidebar footer region, and severity function.

**Phase 7 (Final On-Glass Verification) — COMPLETE, 2026-08-28**, done independently on `main` (PR #18) while this branch executed the 06.x backlog; merged into this branch just now (no code conflict — only this file). Launched at the developer's explicit request ahead of the 06.x backlog. 07-01 (the phase's only plan) executed across 3 tasks: Task 1 (CLI forcing flags + 02-UI-SPEC.md addendum, commit 0cc4ae1); Task 2 (blocking on-glass verification battery, steps A-H, run interactively with the developer over SSH against the real deployed VPS); Task 3 (findings recorded to hardware/BRINGUP-LOG.md, commit 6bd4126). Real findings, not a rubber-stamp: PT Serif Regular legibility, bezel clipping, state distinction, two-flight composition, and wordmark detail all confirmed "parfait" on real glass with no changes needed. Yellow/Red palette confirmed close enough as-is. Blue/Green, however, failed hard on real glass — D-21's sky-tone values had only ever been screen-confirmed, never checked against real ink — triggering two live deviations, both developer-approved in real time: (1) `panel_format.PALETTE_RGB`'s Blue/Green darkened across two iterations (110,180,225)/(140,195,130) → (45,95,155)/(50,105,65), confirmed "c'est parfait"; (2) the flat D-21 background fill replaced with `dither.dithered_state_background()` (Floyd-Steinberg dither toward White, 2-color palette to avoid a real mid-session bug where the 6-color quantizer picked the wrong ink once Blue/Green were both dark) after the developer found even the corrected flat fill too dark at full-panel coverage (simultaneous-contrast effect) — followed by a third fix, `_paint_text_backing()`, painting a clean undithered plate behind every text run after the dithered speckle hurt legibility behind white text. `server/test_poll_loop.py`'s pinned default-config digest was re-pinned twice (documented, same pattern as the prior D-26-outline re-pin). Full suite green throughout (90% coverage). Session also found and fixed an unrelated production issue: the VPS's `inkframe-poll.timer` (legacy, pre-SkyPane-rename) was left running and failing every 30s since a stale mid-migration state, while the real `skypane-poll.timer` was healthy — the legacy timer was stopped and disabled. Carried-forward open items (see `07-01-SUMMARY.md`): A-02-02-01's real +200ft/min departure threshold still unvalidated against real sensor data (visual path only) [CLOSED 2026-09-02 — validated against real production departure data; see Blockers/Concerns.]; the wall-mounted re-check of ROADMAP criteria 1/4 remains open (D-03, not a blocker); ROADMAP criterion 7's "2-3 alternate Blue/Green theme variants for CFG-01" was only partially fulfilled — the single default "sky" theme was corrected, no additional selectable theme variants were built (open for a future phase if wanted); the `inkframe-*`/`skypane-*` naming drift in this plan's own example commands (documentation only).

Phase 06.2 (LED enable/disable toggle) is now COMPLETE (2/2 plans): 06.2-01 shipped the store/endpoint/web-control code; 06.2-02 (the phase's sole remaining plan, a blocking developer checkpoint) is resolved with a full developer sign-off recorded in `06.2-02-SUMMARY.md` — both the cross-process toggle (Part A, 6/6 sub-checks) and the real physical LED on deployed hardware (Part B, including a genuine "still lit" false-alarm report that was investigated, root-caused as expected wake-window firmware behavior, and resolved with no code change) are confirmed.

**06.6.1-03 executed (2026-08-29), the last of Wave 1's 3 parallel-safe plans, run sequentially on this worktree after 06.6.1-01 (CSS tokens) and 06.6.1-02 (History table density).** `companion/pages/health_page.py`'s D-14 anomaly banner dropped its redundant per-anomaly `<ul>` detail list (the Overview tiles already carry the same information via colour) and `ANOMALY_BANNER_TEXT` was re-pointed at the tiles; `collect_anomalies()` itself is untouched (still the sole gate on whether the banner renders), gaining only a docstring sentence. The battery-trend chart moved out of the Overview `dashboard-grid` (now three tiles: Device check-in, ADS-B pipeline, Corroboration) into its own full-width `.battery-trend-section` card below the grid, via new `BATTERY_SECTION_HEADING`/`BATTERY_SECTION_CLASS` constants and `_battery_trend_section_html()`; `_battery_section()` itself was not touched, minimizing collision surface with sibling phase 06.6 (not yet executed, plans to edit that same function's timestamp formatting). A new `_read_health_inputs()` single-sources the five `_safe_query()` reads `render()` and the new public `health_page.anomaly_active(state_dir, now=None)` both consume, so the two can never disagree; `anomaly_active()` routes through the same section builders and `collect_anomalies()` call `render()` uses, wrapped in a broad fail-closed guard (never raises, mirrors `runway_images_available()`'s Phase 06.4 precedent) since `companion/app.py`'s `page_context()` will call it on every authenticated page render once plan 06.6.1-04 threads it in as `ctx["health_anomaly_active"]` for the Health nav-tab notification dot. `companion/test_status_pages.py` grew 36->42 checks (2 corrected — the abnormal-drop detail-copy assertion inverted, the four-tile check rewritten to three tiles + a positioned battery section — plus 6 new). One deviation worth flagging: the plan's own draft assumed `anomaly_active()` returns `False` for a merely-empty-but-writable directory; verified against real execution that this contradicts the codebase's own pre-existing, documented D-12 "never seen -> warn" freshness default (untouched by this plan) — a genuinely empty `state_dir` already shows the anomaly banner via `render()` itself, predating this plan. The test was corrected to assert no-raise + agreement-with-render() for that case instead of a hard-coded `False`. Full details in `06.6.1-03-SUMMARY.md`. `state.update-progress`'s known percent-corruption bug recurred again this session (wrote `percent: 73`, computed from `completed_phases/total_phases` instead of `completed_plans/total_plans`) — corrected by hand to `percent: 89` (56/63), same pattern documented throughout this file's history. `requirements.mark-complete D-02 D-04` returned both as `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent — these are `06.6.1-CONTEXT.md` Decision IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched. `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.1"` confirmed `plan_count: 6, summary_count: 3, status: "In Progress"` (plans 04/05/06 remain).
**06.6.1-04 executed (2026-08-30), the first of Wave 2's plans (depends on 01 CSS + 03's `anomaly_active()` signal, both from Wave 1).** `companion/layout.py` gained the phase's icon system: `ICON_IDS` (exactly five: `icon-device`/`icon-pipeline`/`icon-corroboration`/`icon-battery`/`icon-hamburger`, the last defined here even though plan 06.6.1-05 consumes it, so the sprite stays a single write site), `ICON_DEFS_HTML` (one inline `<svg class="icon-defs"><defs>` sprite, outline glyphs, `stroke="currentColor"`, no external asset), `STAT_TILE_ICON_CLASS`, and `icon_html()` (whitelist-then-render, mirroring `status_dot()`/`stat_tile()`'s own state-value discipline — an unknown id renders nothing). `stat_tile()` gained an `icon=None` keyword (byte-identical when omitted); `page_shell()` emits the sprite once per document. `health_page.py`'s three Overview tiles (Device/Pipeline/Corroboration) each carry their own tinted icon; the Battery trend heading carries the battery icon with no tint class (a page section, not a status tile); Airlines confirmed untouched (`git diff` empty). Three pre-existing `test_status_pages.py` svg-count checks were retargeted onto sparkline-specific markers (`<polyline`/`SPARKLINE_DOT_CLASS`) rather than weakened, since the page now also carries four icon `<svg>` instances. `layout._nav_links()` now also yields the route slug; `sidebar_nav(health_alert=)`/`page_shell(health_alert=)` draw a small error-coloured dot plus a visually-hidden " — attention needed" suffix on the Health sidebar link only, gated on a new `ctx["health_anomaly_active"]` computed once per request in `companion/app.py`'s `page_context()` via `health_page.anomaly_active()` (Phase 06.4's `runway_images`-precedent crossing point, so no nav renderer imports `health_page.py` — `companion/pages/__init__.py`'s boundary held) and threaded into all five live authenticated route blocks plus the unreferenced `_render_tab()` helper; the login/404/preview-error `page_shell()` calls (no `ctx` in scope) were deliberately left alone, correctly defaulting the flag off. `companion/test_companion_app.py` grew 56→62 checks (5 sprite/stat_tile + 1 nav-dot); `companion/test_status_pages.py` grew 42→44 (3 retargeted, 2 new). **06.6.1-05 executed (2026-08-30), Wave 3's sole plan (depends on 01 CSS + 04 icon sprite, both complete), the last CODE plan of the phase.** `companion/static/nav-dropdown.js` shipped as the project's second JS file — same ES5-safe `"use strict"` IIFE conventions as `battery-trend.js`, reading the open state from `aria-expanded` (single source of truth, so the visual and announced states cannot drift) and closing on Escape (scoped to open-only) with focus returned to the toggle. `companion/app.py` gained `NAV_SCRIPT_ROUTE`/`_NAV_DROPDOWN_JS_PATH`, a shared `_serve_script_file()` body (`_serve_battery_trend_script()` reduced to a one-line delegate, `_serve_nav_dropdown_script()` added as its twin), dispatched pre-auth before any session gate. `companion/layout.py` gained `NAV_TOGGLE_ID`/`MOBILE_NAV_ID`/`MOBILE_NAV_OPEN_CLASS`/`NAV_TOGGLE_LABEL`/`NAV_DROPDOWN_SCRIPT_SRC` and `_mobile_nav_html()` (toggle button + always-server-closed dropdown panel, consuming `_nav_links()` exactly like `sidebar_nav()` does); `_nav_html()` (the horizontal strip renderer) deleted outright — not hidden — with D-00's flexbox root-cause reasoning preserved as a removal-rationale comment, superseding that fix rather than re-tuning it; `page_shell()` restructured so the header holds only the wordmark + hamburger nav, with one deferred `<script src="/static/nav-dropdown.js">` before `</body>`, and the theme picker now lives inside the dropdown instead of a third header copy. `companion/test_companion_app.py` grew 62→68 checks: two new (Task 1, pre-auth serving + ES5-dialect), three retargeted at the dropdown (two named by the plan, plus a third — plan 06.6.1-04's single-dot notification check, which the plan's own read_first didn't flag as broken but which needed the same one-dot-per-renderer fix its own Task 2 acceptance criteria already specified), and four new (toggle ARIA/label contract, dropdown contents/order, the three-file Python/CSS/JS DOM-contract guard mirroring 06.5-02's battery-chart precedent, and the no-JS floor). Three Rule-1 deviations, all self-inflicted friction in the plan's own literal `<verify>` scripts rather than any behavioral gap: (1) the plan's own Task 2 gate asserts `'_nav_html' not in src`, which is unsatisfiable together with the plan's own required new symbol name `_mobile_nav_html` (which necessarily contains that substring) — verified the real intent (`'def _nav_html(active):' not in src`) instead; (2) Task 3's own `doc.index(L.MOBILE_NAV_ID)` naively matches the toggle's `aria-controls` attribute (which must legitimately equal the panel's id) before the panel's own `id=` attribute, pulling the toggle's `aria-hidden`-carrying icon into the "panel" slice and tripping the literal `'hidden' not in panel` assertion — the actual test-file checks anchor on the more specific `'id="%s"' % MOBILE_NAV_ID` needle instead, and self-verification used the same corrected needle; (3) the pre-existing notification-dot check retarget described above. Full details, including the exact corrected verification commands run, in `06.6.1-05-SUMMARY.md`. `git diff --stat` across all three task commits touches exactly the plan's four `files_modified`; full suite green except the pre-existing, unrelated `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest-mismatch (confirmed unrelated, zero `server/` files touched). `state.advance-plan` again could not parse this file's prose-based Current Position section (same known limitation); `state.record-metric` needed `--flag value` form rather than the workflow doc's positional-looking example (its own `parseNamedArgs` implementation requires `--phase`/`--plan`/etc., not positional args) — noted here in case the doc needs a fix; `state.update-progress`'s call this session again wrote `percent: 73`/lost `current_phase`'s YAML quoting (`completed_phases/total_phases` bug plus quote-stripping, same pattern documented throughout this file's history) — corrected by hand to `completed_plans: 59`, `percent: 94` (59/63), `current_phase: "06.6.1"` restored. `requirements.mark-complete D-00 D-06` returned both as `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent (these are `06.6.1-CONTEXT.md` Decision IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries). `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.1"` confirmed `plan_count: 6, summary_count: 5, status: "In Progress"` (only plan 06 remains — the phase's blocking human real-device verification checkpoint, which the orchestrator handles interactively rather than an executor agent).

**06.6.2-02 executed (2026-08-31), the second of the phase's 8 plans, closing UXA-15.** `companion/app.py` gained `_POLL_LOCK = threading.Lock()` (process-global, mirrors the existing `LOGIN_THROTTLE` singleton shape) and a rewritten `_handle_poll_now()`: `_POLL_LOCK.acquire(blocking=False)` is the first statement, and the entire cooldown-check → `run_once()` → `mark_poll_triggered()` sequence is wrapped in `try`/`finally: _POLL_LOCK.release()` so a losing concurrent request gets an immediate, honest new `poll_already_running` redirect (never a queued/staggered `run_once()` call) and a failed poll still releases the lock for the next attempt. `companion/pages/config_page.py` gained the matching `FLASH_POLL_ALREADY_RUNNING` constant (distinct from `FLASH_POLL_COOLDOWN` — "executing right now" vs. "triggered recently"), `POLL_SUBMIT_PENDING_TEXT = "Polling…"`, and `_poll_submit_script()` — a new ES5-safe, `_js_literal()`-gated IIFE on the zero-cooldown branch that disables the trigger button and swaps its label on submit, purely cosmetic (the lock is the real boundary, documented as such in the docstring); the zero-cooldown branch now also carries `id="poll-trigger-btn"`, matching the disabled branch's existing id. `companion/test_companion_app.py` grew 69→70 checks: a genuine two-thread concurrent-request test (both threads block on a shared `threading.Event`, released together, against a fresh session with zero cooldown) proving exactly one of two overlapping `POST /poll-now` requests gets `poll_already_running` — not merely asserted by reading the source. `companion/test_config_page.py` grew 37→38: one new check pins `_poll_submit_script()`'s sink-safety/`_js_literal()` discipline. One Rule-1 deviation: two `test_config_page.py` checks (one pre-existing, one this plan's own new required check) used a bare `"disabled" in rendered` whole-document substring search, which false-positived against `_poll_submit_script()`'s own legitimate `btn.disabled = true;` JS property name — both rescoped to a `<button ...>`-tag-scoped regex instead. Full details in `06.6.2-02-SUMMARY.md`. `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports the same single pre-existing, unrelated `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest mismatch documented throughout this file's history (confirmed unrelated — zero `server/` files touched by this plan's three commits). `state.advance-plan` again could not parse this file's prose-based Current Position section (same known limitation documented throughout this file's history); `state.update-progress` correctly computed `percent: 92` (65/71) this time, but a later `state.record-metric`/`state.add-decision` call re-corrupted it back to `82` (`completed_phases/total_phases` = 14/17, the same recurring bug) — corrected by hand back to `92`. `requirements.mark-complete UXA-15` returned `not_found`, consistent with every prior 06.x decimal-phase plan's precedent (`UXA-15` is a `06.6.1-UX-AUDIT.md` finding ID, not a formal REQUIREMENTS.md entry, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched); `UXA-07` — also listed in this plan's frontmatter `requirements` field — was deliberately **not** passed to `requirements.mark-complete` since neither of this plan's two tasks addressed it (it's the programmatic-announcement/`role="status"` finding, genuinely out of this plan's scope; it also appears in `06.6.2-06-PLAN.md`/`06.6.2-08-PLAN.md`, left open for whichever of those actually implements it). `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.6.2"` confirmed `plan_count: 8, summary_count: 2, status: "In Progress"` (plans 03-08 remain).

**06.6.2-03 executed (2026-08-31), the third of the phase's 8 plans (depends on 01's token rework), closing the two structural High-severity findings the audit's own suggested Wave 1 leads with: UXA-01 (desktop-containment half) and UXA-02, fixed jointly with its inseparable no-JS twin UXA-12.** `companion/static/style.css`'s `body` rule gained a guaranteed `overflow-x: hidden;` — the document can never scroll horizontally regardless of any descendant's layout quirk, closing the audit's own measured 287px overflow at 1440x900 with populated History — plus two belt-and-braces defense-in-depth clamps: `.dashboard-main`'s `max-width: 1440px` became `max-width: min(1440px, 100%)` with an added `width: 100%`, and `.data-table-wrap` gained `max-width: 100%` alongside its existing `overflow-x: auto`. Separately, `.mobile-nav`'s clipping half (`max-height`/`overflow`/`transition`) relocated out of the always-applied base rule into a new `.js .mobile-nav`/`.js .mobile-nav--open` rule pair (plus a matching `.js`-scoped `prefers-reduced-motion` override), scoped under a `.js` marker class `companion/static/nav-dropdown.js` now adds to `<html>` as its unconditional first statement — without any JavaScript at all, the base `.mobile-nav` rule alone renders the panel in its natural, unclipped, fully visible flow (UXA-12's floor). `nav-dropdown.js`'s `setOpen()` was rewritten so the native `hidden` attribute genuinely gates the accessibility tree/tab order: opening clears `hidden` before animating in (a hidden element has no box to transition from — synchronous under `prefers-reduced-motion`, via `requestAnimationFrame` otherwise); closing removes the open class immediately and applies `hidden` only once the collapse transition completes (`transitionend`, one-shot listener, or synchronous under reduced motion) — closing UXA-02. `companion/test_companion_app.py`'s `_dropdown_survives_with_javascript_disabled()` was rewritten in place per the plan's own Pitfall 2 guidance (the old assertion — "no `hidden` attribute ever appears" — described the exact pre-fix defect and had to invert to the new correct floor: no `.js` class on the server-rendered `<html>` tag); a new check pins `nav-dropdown.js`'s `.js`-before-lookup string-index ordering and the `hidden`/`transitionend`/`matchMedia`/`prefers-reduced-motion` state machine against `style.css`'s `.js`-scoped rules. `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` 70→71 (71/71 pass). One Rule 1 deviation: the new `nav-dropdown.js` comments initially used backtick-quoted code spans, tripping the file's own pre-existing ES5-safety regression check (no backtick allowed anywhere, guarding against template literals) — backticks removed from the comments, no behavioral change. `git diff --stat` confirmed exactly the plan's three declared `files_modified`; `git diff --stat companion/layout.py` confirmed empty, per the plan's own verification requirement that this plan not touch the panel's server-rendered markup shape. `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports the same single pre-existing, unrelated `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest mismatch documented throughout this file's history (confirmed unrelated — zero `server/` files touched). A new `_TextBBoxSpy` helper (monkeypatches `ImageDraw.textbbox()`'s return value, mirroring `_TextSpy`'s shape) lets the check assert on the real measured bbox rather than re-deriving font-fit logic; the new safe-box invariant (neither line's bbox may cross `SAFE_BOX`'s left edge) is strictly tighter than production's own `_assert_within_canvas()`, which only guards the full 1200px canvas. Verdict: right-padding spread across the sample is 5–12px (7px delta) — **no outlier**, both invariants held for every file. The deliberate overflow demonstration needed the offset raised to 870–900 (not the plan's suggested 400, which stayed 13–73px inside the safe box for every combination tried) to trip the new check while production's own guard still passed — proving the new check catches a real, narrower overflow production code does not. `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` 97→98; `git diff server/plane/render.py` empty for this task, confirmed. A 12-file visual sweep (six airlines × tier-1/tier-3) was generated via `render.build_canvas()` directly (the CLI's `--airline` flag only overrides the main card) into `/tmp/08-05-sweep/`, for plan 08-06's shortlist. **Phase 8 (Panel theme rework) — COMPLETE, 2026-08-31, 6/6 plans.** Implemented spike `001-panel-theme-colours`'s findings: White became the new default theme; the text-backing-plate was removed; `adsbdb`'s `callsign_iata` threaded through so the raw ADS-B callsign never displays, replaced by a four-tier content ladder; the previous card's caption grew 16px->20px with a 20px optical offset correction. Plan 08-06's required blocking on-glass verification session (steps A-H, run interactively over SSH against the real deployed VPS) went well beyond its own D-13 minimum: font weight was reopened mid-session and made theme-conditional (Regular on flat themes, Bold on dithered ones) after uniform Bold read "très agressif" on real ink; a real bug was caught and fixed live (a fixed Blue/Green-tuned dither blend was silently applied to every non-white theme, turning flat Black visibly grey); and the THEMES registry was widened from 5 entries to 11 on explicit developer instruction, with Sky retired outright in favour of fully separate pure/light pairs for every Spectra 6 ink (white, black, grey, yellow, yellow_light, red, red_light, green, green_light, blue, blue_light) — this closes ROADMAP Phase 7's success criterion 7 (originally scoped for "2-3 alternate Blue/Green theme variants"), and also closes Phase 7's carried-forward wall-mounted re-check (D-03) via plan 08-06's Step G. Full findings recorded in `hardware/BRINGUP-LOG.md`'s Phase 8 entry and `08-06-SUMMARY.md`. Post-execution code review (`08-REVIEW.md`) found and fixed two genuine, previously-untested bugs isolated to `draw_source_fault_badge()`: its caption ignored the theme's declared weight (contradicting the phase's own on-glass finding, on the default White theme specifically), and its exclamation-mark dot was drawn as a degenerate zero-length line (Pillow paints that as a single, effectively invisible pixel) rather than a visible filled dot — both fixed, with two new regression checks closing the blind spot (`server/test_render.py` `EXPECTED_CHECK_COUNT` 99->101). `gsd-verifier` confirmed 6/6 must-haves (`08-VERIFICATION.md`), flagging only that ROADMAP.md's goal text ("Black, Yellow, Red and the existing Sky theme") is stale relative to the legitimate 11-theme/no-Sky outcome — not a defect. Carried-forward open items: A-02-02-01's real +200ft/min departure threshold still unvalidated (every real detection observed so far has been an arrival) [CLOSED 2026-09-02 — validated against real production departure data; see Blockers/Concerns.]; DEVICE-05's multi-day battery discharge run, deliberately deferred to end-of-project (Phase 5's sole remaining open item, see "Current focus" above). `panel.bin`'s pinned digest was re-pinned twice more this session from real CI runs (five re-pins total in that file's history), tracking three rounds of render-output movement.

**Phase 9 (Diagonal band theme) — COMPLETE, 2026-09-02, 4/4 plans.** Implemented spike `003-diagonal-band-theme`'s findings: a new dedicated theme family (5 colours — Blue, Blue Light, Green Light, Red, Black — additive to Phase 8's 11, which stay untouched) drawing a diagonal trapezoid band behind the aircraft illustration, a split top label, and a three-tier flight-identifier hierarchy on both cards. Plans 09-01/02/03 (registry, band drawing primitive + top-label split, text hierarchy) executed cleanly with no deviations. Plan 09-04's required blocking on-glass verification session (steps A-E, run interactively over SSH against the real deployed VPS) found real ink disagreeing with the spike's screen preview twice: (1) black text was illegible on every band colour, not just Black as the spike's round 13 fix anticipated — white ink widened from a `band_black`-only exception to unconditional for all 5 band themes, discovered incrementally (Blue found first, Green next, each re-confirmed on glass before generalizing further) and Blue Light's black-ink hypothesis explicitly tried and rejected; (2) the main card's dash rule read too short (24px->48px) and the shared `center_x` anchor moved from the block's top y to its midpoint (the trapezoid's centreline drift was concentrating in the lower two lines). A developer-driven long-name stress test (reusing Phase 7's own fixture) then surfaced two more real bugs beyond the plan's original scope, each requiring explicit developer authorization before being fixed (both out-of-bounds by plan 09-04's own `<in_session_correction_scope>`): the band's three text roles had no shrink-to-fit at all (added `_role_fit_tracked_text_size()` alongside the existing `_role_fit_text_size()`), and the real overflow cause was text fitting against `SAFE_BOX`'s width instead of the diagonal band's own narrower, height-varying width — white text past the band's edge onto White simply vanished rather than clipping, fixed via a new `_band_edges()` helper. A remaining overflow on an artificially extreme fixture led the developer to correctly diagnose it as a data problem, not a rendering one: live-queried against the real `api.adsbdb.com`, confirmed that `municipality` fields for real airports (e.g. Toulon-Hyères, a genuine Orly destination) are `/`-separated compound names — `enrich._primary_city_name()` now reduces these to their first segment project-wide, not just for this theme, fixed in `server/plane/enrich.py` (outside plan 09-04's own declared file scope, also explicitly developer-authorized). All 8 PHASE9-1..8 scope clauses confirmed by `gsd-verifier` (8/8 must-haves, `09-VERIFICATION.md`), reading the real shipped code rather than trusting SUMMARY.md claims, and running all three affected test harnesses directly (`test_config_history.py` 29/29, `test_render.py` 118/118, `test_enrich.py` 52/52). Full findings recorded in `hardware/BRINGUP-LOG.md`'s Phase 9 entry. Carried-forward open items: the frame's mounting state (desk/wall) was not asked about this session, unlike Phase 8's own wall-mounted confirmation; the band-width-aware fit was verified on one band colour/position with long names, not all 5; `_primary_city_name()`'s rule was checked against 2 real airports, not exhaustively. **State at end of this session (2026-08-28), on branch `claude/debug-skypane-0c21a9`:** two more items completed on that branch, both unrelated to Phase 06's companion-app work, merged forward from a session that started as a live-device debugging conversation. Quick task 260828-b0d: corrected an unverified manufacturer citation about panel refresh cadence in `firmware/main/Kconfig.projbuild`, `firmware/main/panel_guard.h`, and `server/poll_loop.py`'s `MAX_STALENESS_S` comment, replacing it with the real Good Display GDEP133C02 datasheet finding (the datasheet's only refresh-frequency guidance is a 24h *minimum* against ghosting, not a maximum-rate wear limit as previously claimed) — zero behavioral/numeric change, proven by pre/post digests. Debug session `illustration-crop-text-margin` (resolved, two passes): the user reported the gap between the aircraft illustration and its flight-info text looked inconsistent across airlines; root cause was `draw_illustration()` anchoring layout to each PNG's full source rectangle rather than its actually-painted (opaque) pixels, while every vendored file carries a soft drop-shadow band the renderer already discards before painting — measured gap varied 17-154px by airline. Pass 1 fixed the vertical text gap (user-confirmed via preview renders); pass 2, requested as a follow-up, fixed horizontal centering (main aircraft up to 7.5px off canvas centre), the previous card's right-alignment (up to 26px between the two aircraft's visible edges), and a previously-unreported previous-card vertical-centering drift (5.5-28.5px, found while re-measuring for pass 2) — all three fixed the same way, via the `IllustrationPlacement.content` tight bbox. Card *sizing* deliberately still derives from the full rectangle so one aircraft's rendered size never depends on which airline preceded it. Neither fix has been confirmed on real Spectra 6 glass yet; both are server-side/comment-only so no firmware reflash is needed once deployed. This also means `server/plane/render.py`'s render output changed again since Phase 06's own digest re-pin (see `06-12`'s CI-fix commit) — a second, independent `_DEFAULT_CONFIG_DIGEST` re-pin landed on `main` for the same reason (real pixel output moved, not a platform artifact this time); reconciled during this merge, see git history for the merged value.

**06.3-04 executed (2026-08-28), turning Health and Airlines into the actual dashboards D-02 asks for.** `health_page.py::render()` replaced its four `_section()` calls with four `layout.stat_tile()` calls (Device check-in, ADS-B pipeline, Battery trend, Corroboration) inside one `<div class="dashboard-grid">` under a new "Overview" heading; `_section()` deleted as dead code. `airlines_page.py::render()` replaced its two `page-section` wrappers with two `layout.stat_tile()` calls (Unresolved prefixes, Resolution statistics) under a new "Coverage" heading, hoisting the existing `coverage_status(rows)` verdict to drive the registry tile's border colour; the statistics tile stays accent-neutral (`status=None`). Every section builder on both pages (`_device_section`/`_pipeline_section`/`_battery_section`/`_corroboration_section`/`_registry_section`/`_stats_section`) is untouched — only its call site changed, and the anomaly banner/source-fault block stay full-width above the Health grid. `companion/test_status_pages.py` grew 25->28 checks; one Rule 1 fix along the way (a new Airlines check's `>Coverage<` substring assertion initially double-matched the pre-existing status-dot label of the same text — tightened to the exact `<h2>` heading markup). `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` reports one pre-existing, unrelated failure in `server/test_poll_loop.py` (42/43, the already-documented `_DEFAULT_CONFIG_DIGEST` re-pin gap — reproduced identically via `git stash` before this plan's commits existed, confirming it predates this plan). `state.advance-plan` again could not parse this file's prose-based Current Position section ("Cannot parse Current Plan or Total Plans in Phase from STATE.md", same known limitation as prior 06.3 plans) and `state.update-progress`'s known percent-corruption bug recurred once more (returned `completed:48/total:57` correctly in its JSON but wrote `percent: 57` — the `total_plans` value — into the frontmatter instead of the correct 84) — both corrected by hand, same pattern documented throughout this file's history. `companion/test_status_pages.py` 28/28, `companion/test_companion_app.py` 55/55.

**06.3-05 executed (2026-08-28) across two sessions, closing Phase 06.3 (5/5 plans complete).** Task 1 (prior session) ran the full suite and reconciled `06.3-VALIDATION.md`'s Per-Task Verification Map against the shipped five-plan breakdown, but found the suite red on a `server/test_poll_loop.py` digest mismatch and paused, returning two open items for the orchestrator: the suite-red blocker and Task 2's browser visual sign-off. Both were resolved this session per explicit orchestrator-provided resolution. **Item 1:** `origin/main` was merged into this branch (commit `8585f69`), bringing in the previously-missing `aeac512` digest re-pin fix; `scripts/run-all-tests.sh` was re-run and all four companion harnesses stayed green (`test_companion_app.py` 55/55, `test_config_page.py` 24/24, `test_status_pages.py` 28/28, `test_view_pages.py` 20/20). One failure remained in `server/test_poll_loop.py` (a *different* `panel.bin` digest mismatch than the one the merge fixed) — confirmed via investigation to be the same category of pre-existing, environment-specific issue (macOS-local Pillow/FreeType rendering vs. the pinned Linux-CI-computed digest), independently confirmed out-of-scope four times this session (`06.2-01-SUMMARY.md`, `06.3-02-SUMMARY.md`, `06.3-04-SUMMARY.md`, this reconciliation) and validated clean by real GitHub Actions CI on this exact branch's prior PR (#16). Accepted as the documented exception; `nyquist_compliant` flipped to `true`. **Item 2:** the 24-item browser visual sign-off (sections A-G, 900px/960px/960-1200px/1440px+/phone width) was performed via browser automation with DOM/CSS/computed-style inspection (screenshots unavailable this session) against a locally-seeded dataset (12 battery readings, 3 runway_events rows seeded into the scratch state dir's `history_db`, since the empty-database state hides the Battery tile's grid behaviour and History's table-scroll fix under empty-state blocks). **Verdict: APPROVED, all 24 items passed, zero defects.** Full verbatim findings recorded in `06.3-05-SUMMARY.md`. `06.3-VALIDATION.md` finalized: `status: complete`, `nyquist_compliant: true`, both Validation Sign-Off checkboxes ticked. `roadmap.update-plan-progress "06.3"` confirmed `plan_count: 5, summary_count: 5, status: "Complete"`. `requirements.mark-complete D-01 D-02 D-03 D-22` returned all four as `not_found`, consistent with 06.3-01's precedent — these are `06.3-CONTEXT.md` Decision IDs, not formal REQUIREMENTS.md entries, so REQUIREMENTS.md is correctly untouched. **Phase 06.3 (Companion UI visual design and desktop layout pass) is now COMPLETE, 5/5 plans.** `current_phase` advanced to `06.4` (Runway picker — not yet planned), the next actionable phase per ROADMAP.md order.

**06.4-01 executed (2026-08-28), closing Phase 06.4 (1/1 plan complete).** `companion/app.py` gained `_RUNWAY_IMAGE_DIR`, `_runway_image_filename()`/`_runway_image_path()`, and `runway_images_available()` — a registry-bounded, never-raising existence check mirroring `gallery_entries()`'s own `try/except OSError` discipline, iterating `device_config.RUNWAY_IDS` (never `os.scandir()`) so the result can never report an image for a non-registry id — wired into `page_context()` as `ctx["runway_images"]`. `companion/pages/config_page.py`'s `runway_fieldset()` gained an `images_available=()` parameter (default preserves every pre-06.4 single-argument call site): each option is now wrapped in `.runway-option`, the number/heading promoted into a Display-size `.runway-option__number` span, with an `<img>` conditionally emitted only for ids present in the set — this module still performs zero filesystem access. `RUNWAY_IMAGE_ROUTE_PREFIX`/`RUNWAY_IMAGE_ALT_TEMPLATE` are defined once in `config_page.py` and rebound in `app.py`, matching the existing `FLASH_KEY_*` precedent. A new session-gated `/runway-image/{id}.png` route in `app.py` validates the requested id against `device_config.RUNWAY_IDS` **before** any path is built (validate-then-join, T-06.4-02), returning an identical 404 for an unknown id and a missing file; `require_session()` is its first statement, deliberately deviating from `/static/style.css`'s unauthenticated precedent (T-06.4-01) — confirmed by re-running `companion/test_companion_app.py`'s pre-existing unauthenticated-stylesheet check unchanged. `companion/static/style.css` gained `.runway-option`/`.runway-option__number`/`.runway-option__image` rules built only from existing tokens, with a `var(--color-border, var(--color-secondary))` fallback so the rule is valid whether or not Phase 06.3 has landed. `companion/static/RUNWAY-IMAGES.md` (new file) is the D-03 drop-in asset contract. show runway number and airport map) is now COMPLETE, 1/1 plan.** `state.advance-plan` again could not parse this file's prose-based Current Position section (same known limitation documented throughout this file's history) and `state.update-progress` recurred its own known percent-corruption bug — both corrected by hand: `completed_plans: 55` (54 + this plan), `percent: 96` (55/57, rounded), `completed_phases: 10` (Phase 06.4 now complete), `current_phase` advanced to `"06.5"` (Interactive battery trend chart — planned, 3 plans, not yet executed), the next actionable phase per ROADMAP.md order.

## Accumulated Context

### Roadmap Evolution

- Phase 4 added (2026-08-25), then renumbered to Phase 3 — "Visual Polish on Real Glass": user asked to split Phase 2 into a functional pass and a design-polish pass; since Phase 2 already built up from basic to polished internally across its 5 plans (02-01 bare flight number → 02-04 route/airline captions) and its only remaining plan (02-05) is pure deployment infra with no visual work, the agreed split instead adds a new phase after real hardware exists, dedicated to refining the already-built design against actual Spectra 6 output — closing the hardware-verified-legibility items every Phase 2 SUMMARY.md carried forward rather than guessed at. Old Phase 3 (Low-Battery Indicator) renumbered to Phase 4.
- 01-08 (battery-life measurement, DEVICE-05) moved from Phase 1 to Phase 4 (2026-08-26), becoming 04-01: user wants the unattended multi-day (up to 21-day) discharge run scheduled at the end of the project, once other phases no longer need this Mac to stay awake continuously, rather than mid-Phase-1. Phase 1's goal/success-criteria trimmed to drop the on-battery-viability criterion (now Phase 4's job); Phase 1 is now 7/7 plans executed. Phase 4 renamed "Battery Life & Low-Battery Indicator", gained requirement DEVICE-05 alongside DEVICE-04 and a new success criterion for the measured mAh-per-cycle figure. Task 1 (checker + pre-registered protocol) was already done under the old numbering and carries over unchanged; only the phase/plan numbers and cross-references were updated. REQUIREMENTS.md's DEVICE-05 checkbox corrected from a stale pre-existing `[x] Complete` (predating this move, predating the actual measurement) to `[ ]` — only Task 1 of 3 is done.
- Phase 5 added (2026-08-26): "CI/CD, Documentation & Legal Compliance" — user flagged this as a missing v1 step during Phase 3 work. GitHub Actions CI (tests + build + code-quality/coverage) with automated (gated) deploy to the real OVH VPS, a README, a LICENSE, and documented third-party API terms-of-use compliance (PRIM/IDFM, ADS-B aggregators) plus consolidated asset attribution. Scoped as its own closing phase, orthogonal to the on-device experience Phases 1-4 build — not yet planned (requirements TBD, pending /gsd-discuss-phase 5).
- Phase 4 (Battery Life & Low-Battery Indicator) and Phase 5 (CI/CD, Documentation & Legal Compliance) swapped (2026-08-26, user request): CI/CD is now Phase 4, Battery Life is now Phase 5. Reason: Battery Life's remaining work (05-01 Tasks 2-3) is deliberately parked until the end of the project — it needs an unattended multi-day discharge run — while CI/CD has no such blocker and can run right after Phase 3. All cross-references renumbered: directories (`04-low-battery-indicator` → `05-low-battery-indicator`, `05-ci-cd-...` → `04-ci-cd-...`), plan/summary/context files (`04-01-*` → `05-01-*`, `05-CONTEXT.md`/`05-DISCUSSION-LOG.md` → `04-CONTEXT.md`/`04-DISCUSSION-LOG.md`), threat IDs (`T-04-01-*` → `T-05-01-*`), `ROADMAP.md`'s two phase entries and Depends-on chain (Phase 4 now depends on Phase 3, Phase 5 now depends on Phase 4), and `REQUIREMENTS.md`'s DEVICE-04/DEVICE-05 phase mapping. `DEVICE-05` itself (the requirement ID) was never touched — only phase/plan numbers moved.
- Phase 6 added (2026-08-26, user request): "Final On-Glass Verification". Phase 3's fourth and final plan (03-04, the on-glass verification battery — RGB calibration, PT Serif legibility, bezel clipping, forced departure/arrival, long names, two-flight composition) was moved out of Phase 3 and became this new phase's sole plan, `06-01-PLAN.md`. Reason, in the user's own words: "cause this will be my really last step" — they want one comprehensive real-hardware sign-off at the very end of the whole project (after Phase 4 CI/CD and Phase 5 Battery Life), not a mid-Phase-3 checkpoint. Renumbered: `03-04-PLAN.md` → `06-final-on-glass-verification/06-01-PLAN.md` (frontmatter `phase`/`plan`/`wave`/`depends_on`, threat IDs `T-03-04-*` → `T-06-01-*`, note ID `N-03-04-01` → `N-06-01-01`, output path → `06-01-SUMMARY.md`). Every genuine cross-reference the plan makes to Phase 3's own artifacts (`03-CONTEXT.md`, `03-UI-SPEC.md`, `03-RESEARCH.md`, `03-03-PLAN.md`) was deliberately left unchanged — this plan verifies Phase 3's shipped design, it just runs later in the project timeline now. `total_phases` becomes 7; `total_plans`/`completed_plans` are unaffected since the plan moved, not was added or removed. (Superseded in part by the next two entries.)
- Phase 6 scope widened (2026-08-26, user request): small font/geometry corrections (a font-weight swap to the already-vendored `PTSerif-Bold.ttf`, a point-size tweak within `fit_text_size()`'s floor, a geometry-constant nudge for bezel clearance) are now applied directly in Task 2's on-glass session and re-verified on the glass, not deferred to a hypothetical future phase — "cause this will be my really last step," small tweaks should happen here. `server/plane/render.py`/`server/test_render.py` added to Task 2's `files`. Only a genuine redesign need (new font family, new illustration asset, materially different composition) still gets recorded as a scope question. Task 3's role narrowed to confirming what Task 2 already applied is committed and documented.
- **Correction (2026-08-26, user request):** Phase 3's four on-glass success criteria (silhouette/illustration legibility, caption legibility, visual DEPARTING/ARRIVING distinction, overall composition) were initially left listed in *both* Phase 3 and Phase 6's ROADMAP sections — Phase 3 kept them with a "closed elsewhere" footnote instead of actually relinquishing them. The user caught this: "je suis un peu perturbé... pour moi la phase trois est complète." Fixed by removing all four criteria from Phase 3 entirely (they now live in Phase 6 alone, in full, not by reference) and reducing Phase 3 to a single criterion it can actually verify by itself — the illustration selection/compositing *mechanism* being correct per the automated suite, independent of how it looks on real glass. **Phase 3 is now marked Complete** (3/3 plans, its one remaining criterion satisfied by the shipped, tested code) — `completed_phases` becomes 3. Phase 6's criteria renumbered 1-9 in full (previously criterion 1 was a vague back-reference to "Phase 3's criteria 1-4"; now they're stated as Phase 6's own criteria directly).
- Phase 6 added (2026-08-27, via `/gsd-phase` after `/gsd-review-backlog` turned out not to apply): "Companion Configuration Web Interface — visual settings, view switching, device health status, and airline-coverage monitoring (CFG-01..05)". User asked to "analyser les seeds et voir laquelle on transforme en phase" — an assistant session first (incorrectly) inferred a different phase topic from stale worktree/branch naming without asking, was corrected, then surveyed all five actual seeds. That survey initially missed one: this worktree's branch (`claude/new-phase-27bddf`) had fallen 2 commits behind `origin/main` (PR #7, `claude/exploration-features-79794e`, adding `.planning/seeds/on-device-fault-icon.md` and REQUIREMENTS.md's CFG-05/DEVICE-06) — fast-forwarded before presenting the corrected 5-seed list. User chose "Interface web compagnon (CFG-01..05)" over the other four (AeroDataBox destination lookup, local RTL-SDR backup, presence-adaptive poll cadence, standalone on-device fault icon DEVICE-06). `/gsd-review-backlog` was tried first since it matches this exact use case by description, but that skill assumes a formal `999.x`-phase-directory + `## Backlog`-section convention this project has never used (deferred ideas live in REQUIREMENTS.md's "v2 Requirements" and `.planning/seeds/*.md` instead) — fell back to the plain `/gsd-phase` add workflow. REQUIREMENTS.md updated by hand alongside it: CFG-01..05 moved from v2 to v1 Requirements (new "Companion Configuration Web Interface" subsection), DEVICE-06 stays behind in v2 under a trimmed "On-Device Fault Fallback" section, Traceability table and coverage counts updated (11 v1 requirements total, up from 6). Not yet discussed or planned — goal/success-criteria TBD, pending `/gsd-discuss-phase 6`.
- **Renumbering (2026-08-27, user request):** The phase above was first added as Phase 7 by `/gsd-phase add`'s default (append to the end, depend on the immediately preceding phase — then Phase 6: Final On-Glass Verification). The user asked why it depended on Phase 6; told honestly that this was the tool's mechanical default and not a real technical or sequencing decision, they pointed out it also contradicted Phase 6's own stated intent to be "the project's true last step... done once everything else is finished," and asked for the web-interface phase to run first. Offered a lower-cost alternative (a decimal insertion, Phase 5.1, matching the precedent already set by Phase 3.1 — leaves the ~30 existing "Phase 6" references in completed phases' own artifacts untouched) versus the literal full renumber; the user chose the full renumber despite the cost being made explicit. Executed: `06-final-on-glass-verification/` ↔ new phase directory swapped (git mv for the tracked one, plain mv for the still-untracked one), `06-01-PLAN.md` → `07-01-PLAN.md` with its own frontmatter/threat-IDs/note-ID renumbered (`T-06-01-*` → `T-07-01-*`, `N-06-01-01` → `N-07-01-01`) and a new "Second Renumbering Note" added alongside its original 2026-08-26 Provenance Note (history preserved, not overwritten — the note now correctly says 03-04 became 06-01 at the time, then 06-01 became 07-01 now). ROADMAP.md fully updated: both phase sections swapped and renumbered in place (each carrying its own "Note on numbering" explaining why), the Phase 3/3.1 sections' live "moved to Phase 6" cross-references corrected to "Phase 7", Depends-on chain updated (new Phase 6 depends on Phase 5, Phase 7 now depends on Phase 6), Progress table and Execution Order line updated. REQUIREMENTS.md's CFG-01..05 references corrected from Phase 7 to Phase 6 throughout. **Deliberately left untouched**, matching this project's own established precedent (STATE.md's history stays append-only; the original Phase 3→6 move likewise left Phase 3's own artifacts alone): `03-VERIFICATION.md`, `03.1-01-PLAN.md`, `03-04-PLAN.md`/`03-04-SUMMARY.md`, and the `260827-hyy-*` quick-task files — these are point-in-time records of already-completed phases, accurate as of when they were written, and rewriting them would corrupt the historical record (e.g. `03.1-01-PLAN.md` cites the literal shell command `grep -c 'Phase 6' .planning/ROADMAP.md` it actually ran on 2026-08-27 — changing the string would misrepresent what was verified that day). An assistant session initially told the user ~8 files needed rewriting before catching this distinction from the Phase 4/5 swap precedent; corrected before touching those files. Net changed: 1 plan file (identity only, no substantive content), ROADMAP.md, REQUIREMENTS.md, this STATE.md entry, and two phase directory names.
- **Phase 6 completed (2026-08-28), backlog promoted to decimal phases 6.1-6.6:** After 06-12's live verification (90-minute real ADS-B capture, live Caddy-log/network-posture confirmation, developer sign-off checkpoint), `gsd-tools query phase.complete "06"` was run. It has a real bug: it also flipped Phase 7's top-level roadmap checkbox to `[x]` and appended a false "(completed 2026-08-28)" — Phase 7 (Final On-Glass Verification) has not been touched. Caught and corrected by hand (Phase 7's own "## Phase Details" section, including its `Plans: 0/1` line, was untouched by the bug — only the top-level summary line was wrong). Separately, six backlog items — the two pre-existing (999.1 battery status, 999.2 LED toggle) plus four raised during 06-12's sign-off checkpoint itself (999.3 UI visual design/desktop layout, 999.4 runway picker number/map, 999.5 interactive battery chart, 999.6 timestamp/copy clarity) — were promoted via `gsd-tools query phase.insert 6 "..."` (called six times) into decimal phases 06.1-06.6, all inserted between Phase 6 and Phase 7 at the user's explicit request ("before phase 7... call it 6.1"), matching the exact reasoning that already got Phase 6 itself reordered ahead of Phase 7. The raw `phase.insert` CLI call does not touch the top-level `## Phases` bullet list (unlike Phase 3.1's insertion, which did) or run the fuller `insert-phase.md` workflow's `state.patch`/`state.add-roadmap-evolution` follow-up steps (the `state.patch` field names it tries — "Current Phase"/"Next recommended run" — don't match this project's STATE.md format, consistent with every other STATE.md field-matching failure already recorded above) — both done by hand instead: the six phases added to the top-level list between Phase 6 and Phase 7, and this entry substituting for the automated evolution log. The old `## Backlog` section and its six `999.x` placeholder directories (each holding only a `.gitkeep`) were deleted — their content now lives in the promoted phases' ROADMAP.md entries. `total_phases` becomes 14 (was 8, +6 insertions); `completed_phases` becomes 6 (Phase 7's erroneous 7th was the bug just described).

Not yet discussed or planned — goal/success-criteria/requirements TBD, pending `/gsd-plan-phase 8` (or `/gsd-discuss-phase 8` first, though the spike's README.md/MANIFEST.md already cover most of what a discuss-phase pass would otherwise need to extract).
- Phase 9 added (2026-09-01, via `/gsd-phase`, branch `claude/amelioration-rendu-tableau-bcd0ce`, immediately followed by `/gsd-plan-phase --chain`): "Diagonal band theme" — implements spike `.planning/spikes/003-diagonal-band-theme/`'s validated findings from a 15-round interactive exploration session (real render-pipeline previews): a new dedicated theme, additive to Phase 8's existing 11 (untouched), drawing a diagonal trapezoid band behind the aircraft illustration in 5 colours (blue, blue-light-dithered, green-light-dithered, red, black); a split top-label tag (airport code merges into the left state label, "RWY 3" alone stays a separate right tag); a three-tier flight-identifier hierarchy (big number / dash rule / tracked route line / airline·type, built entirely from the real, unmodified `_flight_line1_text()`/`_flight_line2_text()` content ladder) centred inside the band for both the main and previous cards, with one computed centre-x shared per block (not recomputed per line — a confirmed spike regression); band-aware ink colour (white text specifically when the band is black, black everywhere else); PT Serif stays Regular project-wide, no italic vendoring; and a required, blocking on-glass verification pass before the phase can close (D-13 precedent), specifically covering a re-render of all four content-ladder tiers and a re-check of the previous card's band clearance against the final round-15 geometry (both predate rounds 9-15's position changes in the spike). Hit the identical `gsd-tools query phase.add` bug as Phase 8 (entry above): the full multi-clause description landed verbatim as the ROADMAP.md heading — shortened by hand to "Diagonal band theme" with the detail moved into the Goal line, and the top-level "## Phases" summary bullet (which `phase.add` doesn't touch) added by hand. Unlike Phase 8's fix, the auto-generated directory slug (`09-new-theme-diagonal-decorative-band-implementing-spike-003-s-`) WAS renamed this time, to `09-diagonal-band-theme` — safe here since the directory held only a `.gitkeep`, created seconds earlier, with nothing yet referencing the old name (Phase 8's caution about desyncing "other GSD state" doesn't apply to a directory nothing has touched yet). Not discussed — going straight to `/gsd-plan-phase` per the developer's own instruction, since the spike's README.md/MANIFEST.md already carry the full decision trail a discuss-phase pass would otherwise extract.

### Decisions

Decisions are logged in PROJECT.md Key Decisions table.
Recent decisions affecting current work:

- Local ADS-B receiver (geofenced to runway 3) chosen over public flight-data/schedule APIs — validate reception early in Phase 1
- Battery-only power for v1, solar deferred — Phase 1 must produce a real mAh/wake-cycle measurement
- Server on a small always-on cloud VPS, not a home server
- No freshness timestamp / stale-data indicator in v1 (explicit user tradeoff, despite research flagging it as a pitfall)
- [Phase 01]: Vendored byos_server.py byte-for-byte from flightportrait/frame @ ce3335fc, adding only a --state-dir flag so the harness's isolated test tokens never collide with the long-running stub instance the hardware plans keep alive
- [Phase 01]: make_test_panel.py's quadrants pattern splits left/right exactly on a byte-pair boundary (column 600 of 1200) so no byte mixes two colours except the two intentional border-edge bytes
- [Phase 01]: Disabled Bluetooth in firmware/sdkconfig.defaults (only functional delta from upstream) - Phase 1 has no BLE provisioning, hardcoded secrets.h credentials replace it
- [Phase 01]: Containerised build (espressif/idf:v5.3.1), native flash - build.sh runs the whole ESP-IDF toolchain in Docker as the invoking user; USB flashing stays on the host for plan 01-06 because Docker Desktop's macOS USB passthrough is unreliable
- [Phase 01]: Vendored+trimmed api_client.c/wifi.c to the three device endpoints and STA join, reading credentials from a gitignored secrets.h
- [Phase 01]: Trimmed nvs_schema.h from ~30 upstream keys to exactly 4: bearer token, image hash, failure counter, boot counter
- [Phase 01]: Added FP_ERR_HTTP_TRANSPORT/STATUS/JSON/FP_ERR_IMAGE_VERIFY sentinels so state_machine.c can emit the exact Log Line Contract step token
- [Phase 01]: Froze the five-line Log Line Contract in firmware/VENDOR.md for plans 01-06/01-07/01-08 to grep
- [Phase 01]: Hardware orders placed — Seeed EE02 kit (order <seeed-order-ref>) and Kubii battery+cable (order <kubii-order-ref>); Unblock Date 2026-08-26 gates only 01-06/01-07, not 01-05 (already complete)
- [Phase 01]: aggregator-sufficient (2026-08-05): both adsb.fi and airplanes.live cleared the coverage bar over ~92min real traffic (38/37 distinct aircraft <=3000ft, 2/2 on-ground); update-cadence miss (36.2s/22.4s vs 15s) judged immaterial given the device's multi-minute refresh cycle. No RTL-SDR hardware ordered; D-02 fallback not invoked.
- [Phase 01]: airplanes.live preferred as primary aggregator provider (tighter update gap, zero sample errors); adsb.fi retained as secondary given near-total hex overlap.
- [Phase 02]: D-P2-01 multi-aircraft selection rule implemented as (effective_altitude_ft, seen_pos, hex) total order, proven deterministic under shuffled input
- [Phase 02]: poll_loop.py hardcodes state=arriving for every detected flight - deliberate stub, marked in code, 02-02 replaces with real D-03 inference
- [Phase 02]: Deferred marking PLANE-01/02/03 complete in REQUIREMENTS.md - this slice only satisfies detect->render->serve mechanics, not the full requirement text (enrichment is 02-04, real state inference is 02-02, hardware-verified legibility is 02-05)
- [Phase 02]: D-P2-04 deadband (+-200 ft/min) implemented with explicit bool rejection; descent side backed by the real EJU84YF flare fixture, climb side documented as provisional/symmetry-derived per A-02-02-01 (02-RESEARCH.md Open Question 2, closed in 02-05's hardware QA) [CLOSED 2026-09-02 — climb side now backed by real production data (thousands of real departure detections via server/history_db.py's runway_events table and journalctl -u skypane-poll counts), not symmetry alone; see Blockers/Concerns.]
- [Phase 02]: A first-ever detection whose vertical rate sits inside the deadband (confirmed_state is None) renders the Empty state rather than guessing a colour
- [Phase 02]: aircraft-silhouette source SVG is detailed 3/4-view line-art (evenodd multi-subpath), not a flat silhouette - cleaned via a from-scratch Pillow dilate/flood-fill/erode pipeline rather than a vector editor — no rsvg-convert/Inkscape/numpy/scipy available in this environment; the flood-fill approach turns any traced line-art into a flat solid mask without hand-editing vector paths
- [Phase 02]: Silhouette sized by fitting within both the 900px width cap and 02-02's existing 260px height cap while preserving the vendored asset's own ~2.22:1 aspect ratio - height cap binds first, leaving 02-02's zone-3 reservation and FLIGHT_NUMBER_TOP_Y untouched
- [Phase 02]: adsbdb.com live-verified at 52.6% real-world hit rate for this airport's traffic mix (worst on low-cost per-tail-rotating callsigns like Transavia's TVF*) - the "Route unavailable" fallback is a designed first-class state (N-02-04-01), not a rare edge case; enrichment cache persists hit *and* miss results in poll_state.json so a rotating callsign is never re-queried
- [Phase 02]: Airline-line Y position computed from fixed font-metric constants only, never from a rendered route line's bbox - guarantees the fallback caption lands at the exact same position as a resolved-route render, no doubled gap
- [Phase 02]: 02-05: deployment target switched from Hetzner CX22 to OVH VPS-1 (same spec, price parity) per explicit user redirect — D-P2-06 explicitly leaves infrastructure specifics to discretion, so treated as a provider substitution, not an architectural change; deploy/ scripts needed zero functional edits since they were already provider-agnostic
- [Phase 02]: 02-05 Task 2 complete: OVH VPS-1 (<public-host>, Ubuntu 26.04) live-provisioned and fully verified - TLS, auth gate, firewall, and real ADS-B pipeline all confirmed against the live host. Fixed 4 live-discovered deploy bugs: sudo-user support, generic python3 packaging, missing adsb-test/runway3.json geofence config, missing Caddy access logging.
- [Phase 02]: 02-05 Task 3 complete (2026-08-26): firmware flashed with the real OVH base URL. Found and fixed a real bug live - the device's NVS partition still held a bearer token from Phase 1 local-stub testing (NVS is untouched by an app-region-only flash), so the first poll against the real server 401'd; fixed by erasing just the NVS region (0x9000/0x6000), forcing re-enrollment. Confirmed end-to-end via server logs (setup 200 -> display 200 -> img 200, hash-matched 960,000 bytes) and developer sign-off on real glass: legibility "clearly legible", edges "hard, flat", layout correct (left-facing arrival, no clipping), real-flight cross-check (DAH1112 from Béjaïa), and a forced enrichment-fallback caption (EJU84YF, via the real render code path) both confirmed. Step 6 (real-departure threshold, A-02-02-01) explicitly deferred by developer choice - every real detection so far has been an arrival; carried forward as an open item for Phase 3. [CLOSED 2026-09-02 — validated against real production departure data; see Blockers/Concerns.] PLANE-01/02/03 now marked complete in REQUIREMENTS.md. Phase 2 closed (5/5 plans).
- [Phase 01]: EE02 board profile verified against real hardware with zero pin/config corrections needed - firmware/sdkconfig.ee02.defaults stays byte-identical to upstream
- [Phase 01]: 'Device appears then disappears on USB' during 01-06 was diagnosed as esp_deep_sleep_start() correctly powering off USB Serial/JTAG outside the RTC domain, not a boot loop/brownout - documented in hardware/BRINGUP-LOG.md
- [Phase 01]: measured panel full-refresh duration ~31.5s (two independent captures) - input for Phase 2 rendering-cadence UX
- [Phase 01]: 01-07 Task 1/2 complete - hardware/logtools.py (stdlib-only stamp/check-backoff/selftest) proven on 3 fixtures before hardware use; real doubling curve (300/600/1200/2400/4800s across backoff_n=0..4) captured on real hardware over an ~80min unattended run and machine-verified. Known low-severity finding: the WiFi SSID leaks into backoff-run.log via ESP-IDF's own wifi component debug line (not the project's Log Line Contract, not the bearer token/password/setup secret) - documented in 01-07-SUMMARY.md, not yet fixed (would need a reflash+recapture).
- [Phase 01]: 01-07 Task 3 (power-cycle persistence proof) deliberately deferred overnight at explicit user request - device left connected (USB) with battery still disconnected, holding backoff_n=5 in NVS; stub server left stopped (Task 3 needs it down through its own step 6); capture loop + caffeinate stopped so the Mac can sleep normally.
- [Phase 01]: 01-07 Task 3 complete: NVS failure counter proven to survive three real physical power cycles (no battery) - backoff_n continued 7->8 across a power-cycle where the wall-clock gap (12m37s vs a 6h/21600s armed sleep) makes an external interruption mathematically certain; recovery poll + follow-up failure proved a success resets to backoff_n=0/sleep_s=300. The literal 'wake reason=power-on' console line could not be captured on any of 3 cold power-ons (diagnosed via macOS kernel USB log as a genuine USB re-enumeration delay on this board's marginal connection, not a capture-script bug or firmware defect); corroborated instead via the device's own X-Boot-Reason=power-on HTTP telemetry header. Full diagnosis in hardware/BACKOFF-OBSERVATION.md. DEVICE-03 marked complete.

- [Phase 05] **Correction (2026-08-27, same session):** the entry above's GPIO10/external-resistor research was wrong, and the developer flatly rejected it anyway ("il n'y a absolument pas question pour moi de faire de la soudure ou de mettre des résistances. Je ne veux pas du tout faire ça"). Real answer, found via fresh WebSearch/WebFetch against Seeed's own docs: this project's hardware is the XIAO ESP32-S3 Plus module soldered onto Seeed's **EE02 driver board**, and the EE02 board itself already has a factory-populated battery-voltage divider wired to `A0 (GPIO1)`, enabled via `D5 (GPIO6)` - documented on Seeed's EE0x cookbook (`wiki.seeedstudio.com/ee04_with_platformio/`, explicitly "Applicable to EE02 / EE03 / EE04 / EE05"), a fact the original research missed because it investigated the bare XIAO module's own docs/forum threads instead of the EE0x driver-board family's documentation. **No soldering, no external resistors, no pre-work continuity check.** Held at MEDIUM-HIGH (not HIGH) confidence only because the cookbook's own worked example specifically names "EE04" rather than "EE02" in that one sentence - resolved cheaply by the plan's own flash-and-observe verification (read the pin, check the reported mV looks like a real battery voltage) rather than by more research. `05-RESEARCH.md`, `05-CONTEXT.md`'s D-03, and `05-VALIDATION.md`'s manual-verification table all corrected in place; `05-03-PLAN.md` fully regenerated by the planner (old version's soldering/resistor/continuity-check task content deleted outright, not merely edited) and re-verified by plan-checker (VERIFICATION PASSED, explicit confirmation zero soldering/resistor/continuity-check references remain). `05-02-PLAN.md` untouched throughout - it has no firmware/hardware content and was never affected.

Separately, the same `state.begin-phase` call that's supposed to just flip `status: executing` clobbered the hand-corrected `Status:`/`Last activity:`/progress-percent content in this file (reverted percent to a stale/wrong 71%, replaced the detailed Status paragraph with a one-line generic string) - reverted via `git checkout` and the `status: executing` change re-applied by hand instead, same "SDK auto-write conflicts with hand-curated narrative content" pattern documented multiple times above in this log.

Task 2: `fp_battery_mv()` (ESP-IDF `adc_oneshot`/`adc_cali` module) enables the EE02 board's factory sense divider, reads it once per wake via a file-scope cache, releases every resource (ADC unit, calibration handle, enable line) on every path including every error return, and `api_client.c`'s `telemetry_headers()` now sends the real reading instead of the compiled-in `"0"` literal. `firmware/build.sh` (the containerised ESP-IDF 5.3.1 build) could not run in this sandbox - no Docker daemon available - so Task 2's ESP-IDF-dependent half is unverified by any build/run in this session, proven only by strict manual review against `05-RESEARCH.md`'s Pattern 1 body, `wifi.c`'s resource-teardown discipline, and `epd13in3e.c`'s GPIO idiom; `sh firmware/tests/run_host_tests.sh` (4/4 suites) and a strict `cc -Wall -Wextra -Werror -std=c11` compile of the pure-math module both pass. One acceptance-criteria grep in the plan itself (`X-Battery-Mv` non-comment filter) was found to be pre-existing-broken (its regex excludes any 2+-space-indented line, so it also returns 0 for the untouched, working `X-Rssi` header line) - not a code defect, documented in `05-03-SUMMARY.md` rather than "fixed" by de-indenting real code. ### Pending Todos
